UEW Card Archive

The Ides of March '99

circa 15 March 1999
Content noticeA period document, served as recovered; withheld details are marked. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
(Fade into the UEW Control Center where Chris Flint is seated. Chris is wearing a nice tuxedo with UEW on it in yellow for the occasion. Behind him is a monitor with the Ides of March graphic on it.) Flint: Good evening ladies and gentlemen and welcome to the single most anticipated event in UEW history. Tonight, we have quite a few surprises in store for you, so sit back, relax and enjoy the show. (The graphics on the monitor change, first to the UEW Mexico logo, then a picture that shows Gambit and Jason Keening side by side.) Flint: Our first section of the show tonight will originate from Mexico City, Mexico. Out opening bout will feature "the Screaming Drillbit" Jason Keening as he takes up the $500,000 challenge of Freddie Scuttle. For those of you who don't know, Freddie Scuttle offered $500,000 to the man who can pin Gambit's shoulders to the mat for a 3 count. As a side note to this match, the two men have requested that strict scientific rules be enforced for this match, so the referee is sure to keep a tight reign on this bout. (The screen comes to life again, and this time it shows a picture first of the UEW Television Tag titles, before disolving to a split screen. On one half of the screen, the Partners in Crime are shown, the other side of the screen is split in half again, and in one portion is a picture of the American Dreams, and the other the Chaos Crew.) Flint: Also tonight, we will see a battle to determine who is the number one contender to the UEW Television Tag titles. That match will feature a rematch of one of the first round matches from the recent Television Tag tournament. On one side is the American Dreams and the other is the Chaos Crew. The winner of the match has the unenviable task of taking on the Partners in Crime. The Partners have steam rolled through the competition in UEW so far, and it will be quite a challenge for someone to step up and win two matches to dethrone the champs. But, this is the UEW, and stranger things have happened. (The screen changes again briefly showing the UEW American title before changing to a shot of Shawn Harrison and Nevermind.) Flint: Our main event of Ides Mexico will feature newly crowned American champion, "Real Deal" Shawn Harrison as he looks to fight off the challenge from former American champion Nevermind. Nevermind could be fighting in front of one of the fed audiences in the world that supports him. Though he was a menacing a rule-breaker even way back then, the Mexican fans consider him one of their own, as he did the vast majority of his training in Merico growing up. (The screen changes again as the Ides Tampa logo comes up, followed by a shot of Nick Payne and Nick Leshenn.) Flint: Our second section tonight will originate from Tampa, Florida. Leading things off tonight will be a big rematch. Several weeks ago on Heat, Nick "Nighthawk" Leshenn scored a rather controversial victory of Professor Payne. Tonight, Payne looks to even the score as the two meet for the second time. Actually, this feud dates back to even before the UEW as the two men had a long running rivalry in the old DWO when Payne was that organizations World champion. (Once again the screen changes, and we see shots of each of the 10 participants in the big elimination tag match before the screen finally settles on shots of Liam Rogan and Brent Maverick.) Flint: In what could be the wildest match we see all night, we will see 10 bright young UEW stars step into the ring for an elimination tag match. On one side, we will see a team of Revolution members led to the ring by Brent Maverick. Brent's team mates will be: the Al Robertson Experience, "Wildcat" Jim Lewis Jr., and Dan "Flash" Kaufman. This is the UEW PPV debut for three-fifths of Team Revolution, but Kaufman and Lewis though new to UEW PPV's have both been around the block a few times. On the other side, Liam Rogan leads his band of men into action. His team features "Drifter" J.D. Carter, Men in Black and the Man With No Name. Apparently, this is the UEW PPV debut for three fifths of Team OutKast as well... though their is some speculation that the Man With No Name is in fact a former UEW competitor who has indeed fought on several UEW PPV's. This is one match you won't want to miss. (The shot changes again. First, it shows the UEW's Cruiserweight title, then it shows a triple screen shot of Kyle Kamikaze and Trixxster with Doc Holliday in the middle.) Flint: From there, we go to the controled madness known as the UEW's cruiser division. Tonight, we will see Doc Holliday defend his Cruiserweight title against Kyle Kamikaze and the Trixxster. (The screen comes to life again, and a quick shot of a tornado is shown followed up by pictures of the UEW Ultimate Tag titles and then the Suicidal Stallion and Explosion.) Flint: In the main event of Ides Tampa, we will see the Explosion as they try to defend their UEW Ultimate Tag titles as they take on former Ultimate champs Suicidal Stallions. (The screen changes, and we see the Ides Greece logo followed by a shot of CandyMan and Rob Magnum.) Flint: The third and final section of tonights show will originate from Athens Greece. In the opening match there, we will see the up and coming superstar CandyMan as he takes on former Ultimate champion "No Worries" Rob Magnum. (The screen changes again, first showing an armored car, then a shot of Prince Judife and Mercenary.) Flint: In a new UEW Original, we will see the first ever Armored Car matchup. A feud that started months ago when Prince Judife attempted to pay for one of Merc's famous hits... with counterfiet money. Needless to say, the Merc didn't take that well, and tonight he will be looking for revenge. (The screen changes again, this time showing a shot of the UEW Television title followed by a ladder, followed by a shot of the TV title hanging over the top of the ladder. Finally, the screen settles on a shot of Chris K and Nicolas Christian.) Flint: From there, we will see two local heros go toe to toe. We have seen a feud slowly building between these two since this past fall. Tonight, all of that gets settled. One man will walk out of here the UEW Television champion, the other... a loser in front of his family and friends. On one side of the ring, we have current champion "Machine" Chris K, and on the other we have "The Puppet Master" Nicolas Christian. (The shot changes again, this time it shows the UEW Ultimate title followed by shots of Alex Martinez and Alex Extreme on either side of Ultimate champion Chris Tyler.) Flint: As if all that weren't enough, tonight we have a three way match for the most sought after title in the world today. The UEW Ultimate title is on the line as champion "Ladykiller" Chris Tyler defends against long time rivals "Black Knight" Alex Martinez and "Mr. Excitement" Alex Extreme. Folks this could very well be match of the year, and we are only in March. This is the one match you don't want to miss. (The logo changes one last time, to a shot of the Ides of March logo.) Flint: Folks, it's time to get this show on the road! Here we go!!! (The scene... Mexico City Auditorium... and it's electric! Thousands of screaming fans in attendance, and they look happy to be here. Most of all, it's NOISY! The fans are raising the roof with their volume, and having a great time. The scene switches to a booth up near the entranceway. We see three men, about to start the hosting section of Ides Mexico. First up, a young man, in tremendous shape. He looks much like the wrestler he is... or was, rather, before a serious injury forced him from competition. He has medium-length brown hair, a smooth face, and is wearing designer reading glasses, and a zebra-striped tanktop. His name, according to the graphic on screen, is Brian Ace. His broadcast colleagues, believe it or not... ... are Vinny Van Dam and Carnage. Yes, Vinny Van Dam and Carnage. Well, Vinny's decked out in a brand new "GOT JOB?" T-shirt and his best pair of cutoff jeans. A clip-on bow tie completes the get-up. Carnage is dressed in his usual insanely-colored gaudy-as-hell luchador outfit. Brian smirks his once-famous smirk, and starts off the show.) Brian Ace: Hello, fans, and welcome to Mexico City. Our original broadcast team had a minor delay... well... OK, they got fired. Something about Jenkins violating the Mano A Mano rule in a company beach volleyball match at Spring Break. And the other guy was caught in the possession of illegal IWC tapes. So, I got called up to sit in, because I was the only available guy who could speak Spanish. And we added the only wrestlers that weren't on the card that claimed they could speak Spanish too. Vinny Van Dam: Yeah. I can order from Taco Bell all by myself now. Carnage: AYO! VATO VATO! ARRIBACHO!! BA: Ummm... WHAT?! Carnage: AYO TOCHACHI! BA: Is it me, or does he just try to SOUND like he's speaking a language? VVD: Um. I think he said... er... I dunno. BA: It's gonna be a long day. But at least there'll be UEW action to make things go along. Like our Main Event of Ides Mexico... the American heavyweight champion, a man who's been overlooked for years here in UEW... "The Real Deal" Shawn Harrison... will take on a man who just doesn't care, Nevermind. VVD: I beat him! I did! REALLY! BA: Oddly enough, that's true, Vinny. VVD: You don't believe me do you? I beat Nevermind! Really! Honest! Ask Mr. Diamond if you don't believe me! BA: I believe you... so, any insights on what Shawn Harrison has to do to duplicate your feat from over one year ago? VVD: Um... uh... yes. When he's leaving the ring after kicking your butt... don't move. BA: Fascinating. In our second title match, the Partners In Crime will face off against the winner of a tag team match we'll ALSO see tonight. The American Dreams and the Chaos Crew will go at it, to see who advances to the title match. VVD: I... um... almost beat Bret Young once. I hit him with, like, two moves... man, I shoulda had him. Carnage: SI SI SI SI SI SI!!! VEYRO GATIRO SHAPIRO!! ODEY-GATA!! VVD: Yeah, see, Carnage thinks so too. BA: The Dreams and the Crew had a short but violent meeting not long ago, in the TV Tag Tournament. The winner will face a FRESH PIC squad, who won that tournament. And our opening contest of Ides is one that should be a classic. VVD: Yeah, last year's Ides opener was so cool. It had, like Godzilla and stuff. Everyone thought Godzilla was just gonna kill everybody cuz he was so big. BA: Um... that was Gigantor. Dark Vanity, about half his size, beat the crap out of him. And our first match has a similar size differential. Gambit, "The Giant Lovah", so he says, is HUGE! And he's got a big mean streak to go with that size. And unlike Gigantor, his brain probably wouldn't fit on the head of a thumbtack. His opponent, "Screaming Drillbit" Jason Keening, is a big man by most standards... but he's tiny next to Gambit. You'd look at this match, and think Keening had no chance. VVD: Mr. Diamond told me that Keening DOESN'T have a chance. And I always believe what Mr. Diamond tells me, because he's the smartest man I know. Carnage: AY AY! AY AY! BA: Oh, Lord. Well, Gambit's a total rookie though! A diamond in the rough... but let's face it, he's still in the rough. Totally unpolished, and going against a consummate professional in Jason Keening, who's been around the world and back, and has a wealth of experience. Really, if you look at credentials, and the fact that this match is to be held under Scientific Rules... you'd think GAMBIT has no chance! VVD: But... first you said Keening had no chance, then you said Gambit had no chance... now I'm confused. BA: There's 500,000 dollars of Fred Scuttle's money on the line. The manager of Gambit is THAT confident that Keening can't win. If he does win, that 500 grand goes to the Make-A-Wish Foundation. But most experts feel that money's already in the Make-A-Wish fund as we speak... because this type of match is nothing Gambit's suited for. VVD: Mr. Scuttle's smart too, he reminds me of Mr. Diamond. BA: Well, that match is up, here on the Ides! ................................................................. "THE GIANT LOVAH" GAMBIT... [Black-and-white slo-motion footage of Gambit grooving to the ring.] The big man is on a big, big roll. [Scenes of Gambit throwing "The Servant" William O'Leary across the ring... planting Nick "The Nighthawk" Leshenn with a powerbomb... throwing several men out of the Cruiserweight battle Royal at Holiday Horror 3... and finally, smashing Johnny "Sexy" Bod on the hood of a car with the Long Way Down jumping chokeslam.] So much so, that Fred Scuttle is going to put his money where his mouth is. [Clip of Fred Scuttle proclaiming his $500,000 challenge.] The big man is in his groove... [Clip of Gambit dancing in a nightclub.] ... but what will happen when somebody takes him out of his element? "SCREAMING DRILLBIT" JASON KEENING... [A black-and-white clip of Jason Keening walking to the ring is shown.] A man who stands up for what he believes in. [A clip of Keening intervening, to save a woman from Gambit's aggressive advances.] And when this man stands, he stands tall. [Scenes of Keening warring with Marcus "The Misery Machine" Manson at Rumble In The Bronx... piledriving Doc Holliday... nailing a Samoan Drop on "The Crippler" Ivan Kolinski... and blasting "Heartbreaker" Steve Wallace with the Drilldown one-man spike piledriver.] But how can he stand taller than a giant? [Scene of Keening and Gambit face-to-face.] One thing is for sure... he has five hundred thousand reasons to do just that. And he's not talking about money. [A clip of Jason Keening at a children's hospital, meeting all the young souls there.] Will the Drillbit put a hole in Fred Scuttle's checkbook? Or is he a Long Way Down on the list of men who've tried? JASON KEENING... GAMBIT... SCIENTIFIC RULES... NEXT!!! ................................................................ <We dissolve down to the UEW announce table where three unfamiliar faces are seen. One man is younger than the others, in his early 30s. He is wearing a black suit with a bright yellow tie and his hair is styled to the side. This is Ricky Herbert. Sitting in the middle of the table is a man with a big bushy red beard, and he is wearing a kilt and a Scottish suit jacket. He is Kilty O'Neil. And sitting on the far right of the table is an overweight balding man. He is wearing a white t-shirt that says "EH?" in big bold letters on it. In front of him sits a pound of back-bacon, a bottle of maple syrup, and a six pack of Molson Canadian. This spectacle is known as Earl "Eh?" MacKenzie.> RICKY HERBERT: Hello everybody, and welcome to your first match-up for Ides of March 1999! My name is Ricky Herbert, and my two collegues here are Kilty O'Neil and Earl MacKenzie. KILTY O'NEIL: <In a VERY strong Scottish accent.> Ach! How did I get stuck with a fat slob like this Canadian over here? EARL MACKENZIE: Eh, I'm not fat. So back off hoser. <Earl grabs his bottle of maple syrup and takes a swig. Kilty looks on in disgust.> RICKY HERBERT: Well fans, in case you haven't noticed, this is our first UEW announcing job. And I couldn't be more proud to be part of an organization like UEW. KILTY O'NEIL: Yes laddy, like-wise! EARL MACKENZIE: Huh? RICKY HERBERT: Well we are here in the Arena Mexico tonight to call the opening bout. A match in which "Scientific Rules" apply. Kilty, how about explaining the rules? KILTY O'NEIL: The rules are a wee bit hard to follow, but basically nothing is legal. It's a sissy match if ya ask me! RICKY HERBERT: Well here is the graphic for you folks at home to take a look at. <A graphic comes up on the screen reading the following.> SCIENTIFIC RULES - this means that *NO* infractions of the rules are allowed whatsoever - chokeholds do not get a five-count for the break as they qualify for instant DQ - facerakes, low blows or thumbs to the eye also lead to instant DQ - use of weapons of any kind leads to instant DQ - countouts get the usual ten-count MANO-A-MANO RULES - anyone UEW employee who interferes in this match is fired $500,000 PRIZE - if Keening wins, Fred Scuttle must give him $500,000 to go towards the Make-A-Wish Foundation <The graphic then fades away.> EARL MACKENZIE: <Bites into his back-bacon.> Mmmmmm...tastes good.... RICKY HERBERT: Ewwww... anyways folks, there are your rules. You have to think these will favor Keening tonight, as Gambit is known to cheat a lot. KILTY O'NEIL: Well I say that Gambit will surprise everybody as he brings that sissy Keening a long way down! RICKY HERBERT: Well right now we are going to a shot in the back. I'm not sure what's going on. Anyways, here we go.... [The scene switches from ringside to a locker room backstage where "Screaming Drillbit" Jason Keening is kneeling in front of a bench on which Michael, the young boy from the Make-A-Wish Foundation, is seated.] Keening: Michael, I'm sorry your Mom isn't here to watch this match but I can promise you one thing, I'm gonna take that big moron out, OK? Michael: I dunno, Jason, she's been acting kinda weird. I'm worried about her. Keening: Well, we know she's here in Mexico, right? I mean, you guys flew down together. She'll show up soon, don't worry. Michael: OK... but shouldn't you be focusing on your match with Gambit? Keening: Don't worry, that big idiot won't know what hit him. [The scene switches back to ringside without either Keening or Michael noticing the camera.] KILTY O'NEIL: Ha-ha! The little lad has lost his mother. What a sissy! RICKY HERBERT: Well let's hope that she shows up sooner or later. But Keening should be focussing on the match, not on the little child. KILTY O'NEIL: The $500,000 stays with Scuttle tonight! EARL MACKENZIE: That's a lot of money. KILTY O'NEIL: Too bad it isn't for you. Ach, you disgust me. <Kilty inches his chair away from Earl.> RICKY HERBERT: Well it looks like the other Rick is in the ring ready for the intros, so let's head down to him. <Shot switches to inside the ring where Ricardo "El Ringo" Announcero is standing. The thousands in Arena Mexico begin stomping their feet in anticipation.> RICARDO "EL RINGO" ANNOUNCERO: Ladies and gentlemen, it is now time for our OPENING BOUT OF THE EVENING!!!!!!!!! CROWD: YEAHHHHHHHHH!!!!!!!!!!! RICARDO "EL RINGO": The following contest is scheduled for one fall, in which Scientific Rules apply. Introducing first..... <"Can't Get Enough of Your Love" by Barry White blasts throughout the Arena Mexico. The crowd immediately begins booing.> RICKY HERBERT: These fans are pumped for some UEW action tonight. KILTY O'NEIL: Ach, I can't hear a word yer saying Ricky! RICARDO: He is accompanied to the ring by "Fabulous" Fred Scuttle and . He hails from Parts Unknown, weighs in at 366 pounds and stands at 7 feet 3 inches. Ladies and gentlemen, here is...... "T H E G I A N T L O V A H" G A M B I T ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! <Fred Scuttle comes out through the curtains to a chorus of boos, and he is then followed by Lady Lucifer. The two smile to each other and begin walking down the aisle. Then about a dozen scantily clad Mexican women come out dancing and they are followed by the "Giant Lovah" Gambit.> EARL MACKENZIE: Hey, if there is one thing better than food, it's women! Mmm mmm, finger lickin' good! KILTY O'NEIL: I'll have to agree with ya on that one fatty. RICKY HERBERT: You guys are outrageous. <Gambit has a huge smile on his face as he makes his way down to the ring as he checks out the dancing women around him. When they make it to the ring each woman gives him a little peck on the cheek, and dance off to the back. Lady Lucifer and Scuttle then enter the ring, and Gambit follows in.> CROWD (in English, no less): GAM-BIT SUCKS!!!! GAM-BIT SUCKS!!!! KILTY O'NEIL: Ignorant Mexican fans. They don't see good talent right in front of them. RICKY HERBERT: They can cheer for whom they like Kilty. KILTY O'NEIL: They're suppose to cheer for who _I_ like! RICKY HERBERT: Oh my... <The sweet sounds of Barry White fade away as the crowd quiets down.> RICARDO: And now his opponent. He hails from Los Angeles, California, weighs in at 272 pounds and stands at 6 feet even. Ladies and gentlemen..... "S C R E A M I N G D R I L L B I T" J A S O N K E E N I N G ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! ! CROWD: YEAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!!!!!!!!!!!! [A solid shower of brilliant white sparks explodes above the arena entrance and begins cascading down to the floor as "Higher Ground" by THE RED HOT CHILI PEPPERS begins playing over the loudspeakers. A loud cheer rises from the audience as a familiar squat, powerful figure steps through the sheet of sparks. Jason Keening raises both muscular arms into the air above his head, short strings of black leather hanging from the bands around his wrists. He is wearing his usual white wrestling pants with silver triangles pointing downward along each outward seam. And he smiles broadly as he jogs down the aisle, slapping hands with fans along the way before he slides into the ring underneath the bottom rope. He rolls quickly to his feet and climbs up in one corner, raising his arms above his head to another loud cheer. From his vantage point, he points down to young Michael, the boy from the Make-A-Wish Foundation, who is seated at ringside with an empty chair beside him.] KILTY O'NEIL: Ha-ha, would you look at that. I guess the poor lad's mother isn't showing up after all. RICKY HERBERT: This is very serious Kilty. She could have had an accident on the way to the arena. I hope we can get some word on her where-abouts. EARL MACKENZIE: <Cracks open a Canadian.> Ah well, I guess she's gone. No big loss. KILTY O'NEIL: I think Earl over here ate her. RICKY HERBERT: We'll get some more word later. But right now the referee is ordering Fred Scuttle and Lady Lucifer back to the dressing room. KILTY O'NEIL: Why? It's not like they'll cheat or anything! RICKY HERBERT: Well Scuttle just left the ring and is pointing at Keening. Lucifer now approaches Gambit, and.... EARL MACKENZIE: Gambit is getting some pre-match loving! RICKY HERBERT: A passionate kiss from Lady Lucifer as she exits the ring. Gambit is now checking her out as she leaves the ring. DING!!!! DING!!!! DING!!!! CROWD: YEAHHHHHHHHHHHHHHHH!!!!!!!!!!!!!! RICKY HERBERT: And Ides of March is underway! Gambit is watching Lady Lucifer as she walks down the aisle, and Keening walks up right behind him. He's waiting for Gambit to turn around! KILTY O'NEIL: He's right behind you ya big... oof! RICKY HERBERT: Keening spins Gambit around and....European uppercut! He catches Gambit off-guard, and delivers another! Keening now with an irish whip on the big man. Gambit bounces off the ropes and Keening goes for a shoulder tackle... KILTY O'NEIL: He didn't even faze the big man! Keening bounces into the ropes once more and...STILL NOTHING! RICKY HERBERT: Gambit just smiles as he grabs Keening and tosses him into the ropes himself. Keening comes off and Gambit swings and misses with a clothesline. He turns around and....Keening grabs his head, jumps up and gives him a vicious knee to the face! EARL MACKENZIE: <glug, glug, glug> RICKY HERBERT: That one staggered the big man. Keening now back Gambit up and sends him into the ropes once more. The Giant Lovah comes off and...DROP TOEHOLD TAKES THE BIG MAN DOWN! KILTY O'NEIL: Ach! How'd he do that? RICKY HERBERT: Keening now gets up to his feet and...SNAP LEGDROP!!! HE NAILED GAMBIT WITH IT! KILTY O'NEIL: This isn't good. That Keening sissy is actually doing well. RICKY HERBERT: Keening now lifts Gambit to his feet and wraps his arms around the big man....BELLY TO BELLY SUPLEX.....NO! KILTY O'NEIL: He can't get the big man up! Gambit reverses the hold and lifts Keening up.....POWERSLAM!!! HE CRUSHED THE WEE LAD! RICKY HERBERT: Gambit is now laughing at the fact Keening couldn't get him up. The big man gets to his feet, and takes a few steps back... BIG SPLASH ONTO KEENING!!! EARL MACKENZIE: <Stops drinking.> That one had to hurt, eh? KILTY O'NEIL: The mighty Gambit is now hooking the leg! This could be it! ONE!!!!!!!! TWO!!!!!!!!!!! T.... RICKY HERBERT: Keening kicks out! This guy is not going to give up that easily. KILTY O'NEIL: Sooner or later he will. Go Gambit! RICKY HERBERT: The big man now gets up to his knees and extends his hand for a chokehold....but stops in time. That would have gotten him disqualified. KILTY O'NEIL: Gambit is playing by the rules tonight. And the man will win tonight as well! RICKY HERBERT: We'll see about that. But Gambit now gets to his feet and brings Keening up as well. The big man now brings Keening over to the corner, and goes to ram his head into the turnbuckle... but Keening blocks it! KILTY O'NEIL: Ach, oh-no! RICKY HERBERT: Gambit tries again, but Keening blocks again! Keening now drives a couple elbows into Gambit's gut, and spins him around into the corner. Keening now pushes Gambit back and....KNIFE EDGE CHOP!!!! CROWD: WHOOOOOOOOOOO!!!!!!!!!!!! KILTY O'NEIL: The Mexicans think they are birds. Ha-ha! EARL MACKENZIE: They're whooing pretty loud eh? RICKY HERBERT: Keening pushes Gambit back once more and... ANOTHER CHOP! CROWD: WHOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!!!!!!!!!! RICKY HERBERT: Keening now whips Gambit across the ring into the opposite corner. The big man crashes in and Keening follows..... BUT MISSES WITH THE AVALANCHE!!! KILTY O'NEIL: Yes! Way to go Gambit! RICKY HERBERT: Gambit now spins Keening around and delivers a chop of his own! CROWD: WHOOOOOOOOOOOOOO!!!!!!!! EARL MACKENZIE: All this whooing is making me sick. KILTY O'NEIL: Looking at you eat makes me sick. RICKY HERBERT: You two bickering makes me sick! Sheesh. Gambit now scoops Keening up on his shoulder and walks to the center of the ring....SAMOAN DROP!!!!! HE CRUSHED KEENING! KILTY O'NEIL: Gambit! Gambit! Gambit! RICKY HERBERT: I'm surprised the big man has followed the rules thus far. KILTY O'NEIL: He wants the win. Is there anything wrong with that my friend? RICKY HERBERT: It's just surprising. The big man goes over to Keening's legs now, and grabs each. EARL MACKENZIE: Uh-oh, make a wish! RICKY HERBERT: Gambit has Keening by the leg's, he could get disqualified.....OH MY!!! HE ACTUALLY PUT KEENING INTO A BOSTON CRAB!!! AMAZING!!!! KILTY O'NEIL: YES! YES! BREAK THE SISSY'S BACK! RICKY HERBERT: THE SEVEN FOOT GAMBIT HAS JASON KEENING IN A BOSTON CRAB!!!! HE'S LEANING BACK APPLYING PRESSURE! FARTHER AND FARTHER BACK!!! EARL MACKENZIE: THIS MOMENT DESERVES A DRINK! <Takes a swig of maple syrup> KILTY O'NEIL: AH CAN SEE IT! GAMBIT BEATS KEENING!!!! RICKY HERBERT: KEENING IS CRAWLING TOWARDS THE ROPES TRYING TO BREAK THE HOLD! INCH BY INCH! HE'S GETTING CLOSER!!! EARL MACKENZIE: HE NEEDS TO APPLY MORE PRESSURE! KILTY O'NEIL: HE'S GIVING AS MUCH AS HE CAN! HE CAN'T GIVE NO MORE! RICKY HERBERT: KEENING IS ALMOST AT THE ROPES! HE REACHES OUT AND.... HE'S GOT AHOLD OF THEM!!!! GAMBIT MUST BREAK THE HOLD!!! KILTY O'NEIL: Ach, I thought he had it there. RICKY HERBERT: Gambit immediately breaks the hold, surprisingly abiding by the matches rules. KILTY O'NEIL: You really think he's a cheater don't ya Ricky? RICKY HERBERT: I'm just surprised, that's all. But Gambit is now getting to his feet, and Keening is just trying to fight off the pain from that Boston crab. EARL MACKENZIE: You think so eh? Well a swig of maple syrup will cure what ails ya! KILTY O'NEIL: Oh lord, that is putrid! RICKY HERBERT: Gambit now goes over to Keening and lifts him to his feet. Gambit now with a open-fist hit on the Screaming Drillbit... KILTY O'NEIL: The Screaming Dribble? RICKY HERBERT: Gambit now whips the Screaming DRILLBIT into the ropes. Keening comes off and.....GORILLA PRESS FROM THE BIG MAN!!!! EARL MACKENZIE: Woah! He's up pretty high eh? KILTY O'NEIL: Yes he is, laddy. Now toss that bum down Gambit! Bruise: The decision is now official, he took a beating, but Chris Tyler has successfully defended his Ultimate title against Alex Extreme and Alex Martinez, in HIGHLY debatable fashion! Even I'll say your man was robbed, Rick... though he'd have done the same himself if he could have! Styles: So that makes the Ides of March officially a part of history now. Bruise: So it would seem, as we watch the NA leave the arena through the crowd, what a night of action we've seen in the three locations around the world! surprises, action and...HEY WAIT A MINUTE...IT DOESN'T LOOK LIKE THE SURPRISES ARE OVER YET!! Styles: The two masked men are pulling off their masks, I guess we're about to find out who they are. Bruise: They do, indeed remove the masks... AND IT'S PRINCE JUDIFE AND "THE PUPPET MASTER" NICHOLAS CHRISTIAN!! They remove their black shirts... AND REVEAL ORGANIZED CHAOS T-SHIRTS!! JUDIFE AND CHRISTIAN ARE THE NEWEST MEMBERS OF ORGANIZED CHAOS!! Styles: And they certainly made their presence felt here tonight as part of that stable... WOAH! Bruise: The victory celebration was just cut short! Alex Martinez attacks Tyler! And the New Alliance is right behind him! There's a donnybrook in the ring! Styles: This is UEW, isn't there always? Bruise: Tyler and Martinez continue to throw shots at one another, while Kamikaze and the Stallions exchange with Judife and Christian! And THAT'S NOT ALL! Styles: Extreme's back... with... REVOLUTION?! Bruise: Alex Extreme is hitting the ring, and "The Machine" Chris K, CandyMan, "Wildcat" Jim Lewis, Jr., and "Screaming Drillbit" Jason Keening, who must have made the flight in, are right behind him! Revolution hits the ring, and they start fighting with Organized Chaos and the New Alliance! Even Kolinski is getting involved, because Alex Martinez took a swing at him, too! Styles: Good, he deserved it! That incompetent Russian cost the "Black Knight" his chance at the gold, and I know full well that those don't come easy. Bruise: CandyMan and Judife are grappling! K and Christian are fighting it out! Keening and Mad Bronco are launching powerful shots at one another! Crazy Horse and Jim Lewis, Jr. are running all over the place taking hit-and-run shots on each other! And Martinez is fighting Kolinski, Extreme is fighting Tyler... now Tyler is fighting Martinez... Kolinski throws a punch at Kamikaze for old times sake... Styles: The match is over but the hostility is not. Here in UEW, the hostility never ends, Bruise. They'll all fight until the end! Bruise: IT TAKES THE GREECE NATIONAL GUARD TO BREAK THIS MAYHEM UP! And there are guys who are still trying to get at each other, even with soldiers in the way! ( A stir eminates from the crowd as El Puerco busts through the curtains and starts sprinting down to ringside.) Styles: What the hell does Rogan think he's doing?! He has no business here, we all know it's him under that mask. Bruise: El Puerco slides under the bottom rope into the ring...here comes Judife to greet him...DIAMOND CUTTER BY EL PUERCO!! Styles: Wait a second... Bruise: Christian now... ANOTHER DIAMOND CUTTER BY EL PUERCO!! ALL EYES IN THE RING HAVE DIVERTED THEIR ATTENTION TO EL PUERCO!! Styles: Martinez slowly getting up...CHOKESLAM ON EXTREME!!! Bruise: TYLER TURNS AROUND... MARTINEZ GRABS HIM BY THE THROAT.... ___CHOKESLAM___!! CANDYMAN AND CHRIS K RUSH MARTINEZ AND EL PUERCO RESPECTIVELY... ( ROOF THUMPING POP!! ) MARTINEZ WITH THE CHOKESLAMM!!! EL PUERCO WITH THE DIAMOND CUTTER!!! THE TWO MEN ARE BACKING UP...NEITHER OF THEM REALIZE HOW CLOSE THEY ARE TO THE OTHER MAN!! Styles: The Stallions hop on the apron as does Jason Keening and Jim Lewis Jr. Tyler, Extreme, Candyman, Judife, Christian, K are all laid out in the middle of the ring..El Puerco and Martinez continue backing into each other. Bruise: MARTINEZ AND EL PUERCO BUMP BACKS!! THEY TURN AROUND AND FACE EACH OTHER... MARTINEZ REACHES FOR THE MASK... HE YANKS IT OFF!!! ( ______EARTH SHATTERING POP______) Styles: I knew it was him... BRUISE: OH MY GOD... IT'S JEFF "MADFOX" MATTHEWS!!!! ("Time Of Your Life" by Greenday starts up, and still photos of the evening's card show, as the closing credits roll.)
